### 2026-09-23 04:24:17  #MKT News

【US billionaire Boehly, backed by US government and Gulf capital, bids for Lukoil international assets】Todd Boehly, U.S. billionaire and former Chelsea chairman, is leading a bid for Russian oil company Lukoil’s international assets with backing from the U.S. government and Gulf capital reportedly linked to the Trump family. The assets, placed for sale after Lukoil faced U.S. sanctions last year, were written down to about $20 bln in March and span oil and gas fields from Central Asia to Mexico, thousands of service stations and substantial European refining capacity. Insiders say Boehly’s consortium includes the U.S. International Development Finance Corp. (DFC), which would take roughly a 15% stake, UAE presidential brother Sheikh Tahnoon and Qatar’s Al-Hayat family; Boehly has been preparing for months and seeks to displace U.S. buyout giant Carlyle, which had earlier agreed to acquire the assets. Boehly has not finalized terms with Lukoil; the proposal is advanced but the parties’ negotiation status is unclear.

### 2026-09-23 04:20:47  #MKT News #Star Stocks #Microsoft #Market Themes #Magnificent 7 #MSFT.O #Impact mixed

【SEC considers tightening shareholder-proposal rules; Microsoft to keep current submission threshold for one year】Microsoft (MSFT.O) will continue to consider shareholder proposals under its existing submission threshold through next year’s annual meeting under a one-year agreement with investor-rights activist Paul Chesser. The move comes as the SEC weighs tightening shareholder-proposal rules—changes critics say would marginalize retail investors, religious groups and unions—and could blunt efforts to shift power from shareholders to executives. Microsoft said maintaining the current eligibility threshold for one year will provide a clear, predictable process for the next proxy cycle.

【歐盟宣佈將對俄製裁延長三年】歐盟理事會當地時間22日釋出公報稱，當天歐盟將針對3000多個俄羅斯個人和實體的制裁措施再延長36個月，至2029年9月22日。這些制裁措施包括針對個人的旅行限制、資產凍結，以及禁止向名單上的個人和實體提供資金或其他經濟資源。自2022年2月24日俄烏衝突爆發以來，歐盟大幅擴大了對俄製裁，對此，俄羅斯都採取了反制措施。歐盟對俄製裁效果在歐盟內部遭到普遍質疑。（央視）

### 2026-09-23 04:14:30  #MKT News #DXY #Impact bullish #EURUSD #Impact bearish #WTI

【EU extends Russia sanctions by 36 months to Sept. 22, 2029】The EU Council said on the 22nd it will extend sanctions on more than 3,000 Russian individuals and entities for 36 months, until Sept. 22, 2029. Measures include travel bans, asset freezes and prohibitions on supplying funds or other economic resources to listed targets. The Council noted the sanctions expansion followed Russia's Feb. 24, 2022 military action and that Moscow has implemented countermeasures; the effectiveness of EU sanctions has been widely questioned within the bloc.

### 2026-09-23 04:12:19  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Stocks Close Mixed Amid Yields Rebound — US stock indices closed mixed on Tuesday as the rebound in Treasury yields limited gains from chipmakers. The S&P 500 closed flat, and the Nasdaq 100 rose 0.8% to a record 30,732, while the Dow added 185 points. Another strong session for the AI trade lifted AI infrastructure companies, with the latest catalyst being the strong reception for Meta’s Muse AI agent. Meanwhile, Anthropic is releasing a new AI model designed to handle a wide range of business tasks at a lower cost, ahead of its highly anticipated Wall Street debut. Heavyweight chipmakers lifted the Nasdaq, with Nvidia up 0.7%, Micron rising 5%, and Sandisk jumping 6.8% following an upgrade from Rosenblatt. The S&P 500 pared earlier losses after President Trump said US officials had a “very good” meeting with Iranian representatives. Manufacturer stocks gained, with Applied Materials up 1.8%. Meanwhile, a selloff in bank shares weighed on the Dow, with JPMorgan falling 3.4% and Bank of America dropping 3%.

【Axios: Trump adviser blames high gasoline prices on refining capacity constraints】Jarrod Agen told Axios gasoline affordability remains President Trump’s top concern. AAA put the US average gasoline price at about $4.48/gal versus $3.18/gal a year earlier. Agen said crude "has begun to move," though it is unclear how much is transiting the Strait of Hormuz, and that market crude supply has increased. He attributed price declines to tight refining capacity domestically and globally, citing Russia’s refining output down roughly 30–40%. Agen reiterated the president’s long-term goal is to prevent Iran from acquiring nuclear capability, saying that outcome justifies policy costs over time.

【葉門政府軍稱打死打傷數十名胡塞武裝人員】亞丁訊息：葉門政府地方官員22日說，葉門政府軍與胡塞武裝當天在西南部塔伊茲省再次發生激烈交戰，造成至少16人死亡、數十人受傷。 葉門塔伊茲省西部沙馬亞塔因區區長阿卜杜勒阿齊茲·謝巴尼告訴新華社記者，政府軍當天在該地區擊退胡塞武裝的一次進攻，打死12名胡塞武裝人員，另外打傷數十人。交戰期間，政府軍還俘獲兩名胡塞武裝人員。 謝巴尼說，政府軍方面有4名士兵在交戰中身亡，另有數人受傷。交戰後，政府軍將胡塞武裝逐出沙馬亞塔因的一處關鍵區域。 此外，葉門政府軍發言人馬吉德·努扎伊利當天在社交媒體發表宣告說，政府軍空襲了塔伊茲省紅海港口城市穆哈以及葉門中部貝達省的胡塞武裝軍事裝備和人員。 另據胡塞武裝當天在該組織控制的馬西拉電視臺發表宣告說，該組織近期在葉門西部沿海地區，特別是塔伊茲省西南部和荷臺達省西部開展的軍事行動，已造成超過600名政府軍人員死傷、數百名政府軍人員被俘。胡塞武裝繳獲大量輕型、中型和重型武器。(新華社)

### 2026-09-23 01:14:48  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Tin Rebounds from 2-Month Low — Tin futures in the UK rose to $54,000 per tonne, rebounding from the over-two-month low of $51,880 on September 14th, as the recovery in the AI trade improved the outlook for global tin demand. Strong utilization rates from AI agents released by Meta drove equities for chip producers and datacenter developers to rally, lifting tin contracts due to their close cointeration this year. Tin consumption remained underpinned by electrification projects and AI infrastructure development due to the metal's broad utility in datacenters and battery storage facilities. The metal's soldering capabilities, which are useful in precision soldering in AI infrastructure, prompted industry players to signal that tin demand in AI servers should triple by 2030. Meanwhile, supply from major producer Indonesia remained low as Jakarta pulled back on the issuance of export licenses. On top of that, Jakarta further tightened mining permits and seized 500 tonnes of metal from mines without licences.

### 2026-09-23 00:46:22  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Iron Ore Falls Approaches 1-Year Low — Iron ore futures in China dropped to under CNY 710 per tonne, approaching the one-year low of CNY 695 in early August as low demand and risks on financing trade. Iron ore trading giant Radiant world disclosed $870 million outstanding to six different trade financing firms, detailing the exposure of different creditors to the firm accused of fabricating invoices to secure funding, which has currently not been paid back. The crisis to the major trader halted a large source of physical iron ore liquidity. On top of halting a sector of the trading market, the developments are also due to tighten funding rules for other traders, having already been responsible for the sharp decline in ore futures in late July. Meanwhile, softening demand for construction and manufacturing in China also dented ferrous metal buying. New yuan loans were sharply under historical averages in August, and the construction PMI fell to a record low.

Gasoline Rises to Four-Month High — US gasoline futures held above $3.50 a gallon, its highest level since May 21st, following President Trump's remarks at the UN on the conflict with Iran. The US President stated that a deal with Iran could be reached with Tehran after the US midterm elections. Earlier in the session, Iran had proposed reopening shipping through the Strait of Hormuz within seven days, although contingent on the US lifting its blockade. Meanwhile, the crack spread rose to record highs amid widening refining margins due to rising refined fuel prices. As winter approaches and heating demand rises, refiners are shifting output toward higher-priced diesel, tightening gasoline markets. Persistent Ukrainian attacks on Russian refining infrastructure have also contributed to elevated prices, with several refineries either fully or partially shut down. As a result, Russia is set to extend its diesel export ban beyond September.

Discovery after litigation tied to the deal was settled, people familiar with the matter said. Bank of America, CITIGROUP and Apollo Global Management previously underwrote one of the largest leveraged buyout debt packages on record and drew strong investor interest before the deal was paused. With the transaction now cleared to close, bankers plan to start sales in the coming weeks. Brent crude hovered near $100 a barrel, while President Donald Trump said he expected the US to reach a deal with Iran after the election and noted that oil flows had increased since the conflict began, with prices likely to fall sharply once it ends. Banks weakened, with HSBC down 1.4%, Barclays 0.9% and Lloyds 1.1%, while defence stocks Rolls-Royce and BAE Systems fell 1.4% and 2.6%, respectively. On the other hand, Kingfisher surged 12% after raising profit guidance on strong momentum at Screwfix, Poland and Iberia. Smiths Group gained almost 8% on higher revenue and growth expectations at John Crane and Flex-Tek. ### 2026-09-22 23:28:55  #市場 #焦點 #國際

【特朗普聯合國大會講話要點一覽】
1、介紹任期內美國經濟、能源和投資處於前所未有水平：美國新增了超過100萬個就業崗位，國內貧困率降至歷史最低水平。股市創下了前所未有的水平。現在在美國投資的資金比在其他國家投資的任何時候都要多。在過去16個月中，沒有無證移民被允許進入美國。美國軍隊是世界上最強大的軍隊，過去12個月中投資了1.5萬億美元於美國軍隊。結束了8場持續了數十年的戰爭。
2、關於伊朗戰爭：敦促伊朗達成協議，絕不會允許伊朗擁有核武器。稱在伊朗問題上“有一個重大決定要做”。相信將在美國中期選舉後與伊朗達成協議。呼籲所有國家對伊朗實施經濟孤立。重大變化可能迅速發生，伊朗衝突結束後，油價將跌至衝突前水平以下。正在考慮兩種選擇，一個是與伊朗達成協議，或者把伊朗“送進地獄”。
3、關於俄烏衝突：俄烏和平協議也會達成，比大家預期的要更快結束。
4、關於古巴：古巴是一個“失敗的國家”，將會崩潰。國務卿魯比奧正在與古巴進行深入談判。正在尋求對古巴進行“根本性改變”。

6、關於墨西哥：美墨關係取得了重大進展。
7、關於人工智慧：美國政府檔案今後將使用“超級智慧”（super intelligence）這一說法取代“人工智慧”（artificial intelligence）。反對任何試圖控制人工智慧的全球性“圖謀”。將鼓勵超級智慧，而非限制。

### 2026-09-22 22:48:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #Morocco #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Morocco Maintains Policy Rate at 2.25% — The National Bank of Morocco left its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 2.25% at its September 2026 meeting, marking a sixth consecutive hold and keeping borrowing costs at their lowest level since 2022. Policymakers stated that the impact of elevated energy prices remained limited, mitigated by subsidies on road transport and the continued maintenance of gas and electricity prices. As food prices declined, inflation remained low, averaging 0.3% during the first eight months of the year. Agricultural output is expected to rebound by 16% in 2026, while non-agricultural activity is forecast to slow to 3.1%. Looking ahead, inflation is expected to average 0.7% in 2026 before rising to 1.5% in 2027, driven by an acceleration in core inflation to 2.2% as the contraction in food prices is offset by higher imported inflation. Economic growth is expected to slow to 4.4% in 2026 and 2.9% in 2027, amid a projected 7.6% contraction in agricultural output.

### 2026-09-22 22:20:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Richmond Fed Services Index #Importance 1

US Fifth District Services Index Flat in September — The Richmond Fed Fifth District Service Sector Survey was flat in September 2026, with the revenues index rising to 0 from -8, while the demand index inched down to 2 from 3. Current business conditions improved, with the local business conditions index rising to -8 in September from -12 in August, while the future conditions index fell to 0 from 9. The current employment index fell to 2 in September from 8 in August, while the forward-looking employment index edged lower but remained positive. On prices, the average growth rate of prices paid decreased somewhat in September, while prices received increased a bit. Over the next 12 months, firms expected growth in prices paid to remain broadly stable, while anticipating a slight moderation in prices received. Looking ahead, firms remained relatively optimistic, as expectations for future revenues, demand and local conditions stayed firmly positive.

### 2026-09-22 22:18:17  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Richmond Fed Manufacturing Index #Importance 2

US Fifth District Manufacturing Unexpectedly Declines — The Federal Reserve’s Fifth District manufacturing index fell to -2 in September 2026, marking its first negative reading in six months, down from 4 in August and contrasting with market expectations of an expansion in activity at 5. Both shipments and the volume of new orders fell into negative territory, to -5 and -6, respectively, while the order backlog deteriorated further, falling to -10 from -7. Meanwhile, the employment index rose to 7 from -2 in the previous month. Over the next six months, expectations for shipments (33 vs 26) and the volume of new orders (32) remained firmly in expansionary territory, although sentiment weakened for capital expenditures (-1 vs 4) and raw material inventories (-1 vs 3). Prices paid and received are expected to decline over the next 12 months, while employment expectations dropped notably to 8 from 20. Meanwhile, the future local business conditions index continued to decline, falling to 10 from 16.

### 2026-09-22 21:57:17  #公司

【AI計費模式切換引企業不滿 亞馬遜微軟等以折扣挽留客戶】亞馬遜、微軟、Figma、Workday等軟體企業與雲服務商，正面向客戶及諮詢合作伙伴推出AI產品新折扣方案。客戶此前受定價模式變動困擾，對此已疲憊不堪。客戶此前上調預算採購Anthropic的Claude Code、OpenAI的Codex等產品，同時削減其他開支，來為這筆新增支出買單。如今企業在選購AI工具時變得更加謹慎。軟體廠商改為按照使用量或完成任務數量收取AI服務費，可能推高成本，不少企業對此頗有怨言。舉例來說，一家市值超400億美元的跨國製造企業高管表示：微軟調整GitHub Copilot這款程式碼助手的計費方式、改為按量收費後，向該公司提供了至少一個月的寬限期，企業在此期間仍可以按席位訂閱制付費。此次定價調整後，這家制造企業每月需要為員工使用該工具，額外向微軟支付約15萬美元。按量計費政策落地後，微軟還連續三個月，每月向該企業贈送數萬美元的免費額度。但這名高管稱，受此次計費變更影響，團隊正在考慮將程式碼開發工作遷移至Anthropic的Claude Code以及SpaceX旗下Cursor。

US Natgas Prices Edge Up on Tuesday — US natural gas prices fluctuated between $2.8 and $2.9 per million British thermal units as traders assessed production, storage and weather trends. Average Lower 48 output reached 113.0 billion cubic feet per day in September, above August’s monthly record of 112.2 bcfd, although daily production was expected to fall to an 11-week low of 109.8 bcfd on Tuesday. Strong output and mild spring weather kept inventories above the five-year average, but higher summer temperatures increased gas demand for power generation and reduced the surplus. Analysts estimated storage was 3% above normal for the week ended September 18, down from 3.7% previously. Weather forecasts now point to mostly normal conditions through October 7. Meanwhile, average gas flows to the nine major US LNG export plants rose to 18 bcfd in September from 17.2 bcfd in August, despite the temporary shutdown of the 0.8-bcfd Cove Point LNG facility for planned maintenance.

TSX Advances as Oil Prices Extend Losses — The S&P/TSX Composite Index rose more than 0.5% to trade above 36,000 on Tuesday as oil prices extended their losses ahead of potential US-Iran talks at this week’s UN meeting. Iran reportedly offered to reopen the Strait of Hormuz within seven days if the US takes initial steps to ease military pressure. The pullback in the oil rally, which has fueled inflationary pressures and prompted major central banks to raise interest rates, lent support to credit-sensitive stocks. RBC, TD Bank, and Scotiabank gained nearly 0.5%. On the other hand, Bank of Canada Governor Tiff Macklem warned on Monday that US tariffs could push fourth-quarter growth below 1%, adding that future rate decisions will need to balance slowing growth against surging energy costs stemming from the Iran conflict. Meanwhile, miners were mostly higher despite wavering gold prices, with Agnico Eagle and Barrick gaining about 1%. In contrast, energy producers posted losses amid falling oil prices.

### 2026-09-22 21:47:29  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Coal Eases from 3-Month High — Thermal coal futures exported out of Australia fell to $145 per tonne from the three-month high of $149 on September 7th as higher output from China momentarily offset the recent jump in demand. Bodies operated by the Chinese central government issued a joint notice for local miners to maintain ample output after local prices climbed to multi-year highs. Domestic thermal coal production had fallen since May after an accident at the Shanxi province prompted a series of safety inspections in the sector. This coincided with strong demand among major consumers of Australian coal grades, including Japan and South Korea, as soaring prices for LNG drove utilities to increase capacity at coal power plants. LNG supply has remained at recent peaks as the war in the Middle East halted the flow of tankers from GCC countries. Consistently, the EIA revised forecasts to reflect a 1.2% increase in coal demand this year.

Ibovespa Dips After Copom Minutes — The Ibovespa fell nearly 1% to the 185,000 mark on Tuesday following the release of the minutes from Copom’s latest meeting. The committee noted that although monetary policy is contributing decisively to the disinflation process, price growth remains supported by strong aggregate demand, requiring monetary policy to remain restrictive. Policymakers also pointed out that economic activity has continued to moderate. Major banks posted losses, with Itaú and Bradesco down nearly 1.5%. Petrobras shed 1% on lower oil prices. Other notable laggards included Ambev (-1%) and Rede D’Or (-1%). Vale and Axia limited losses as Vale confirmed the acquisition of a 30% stake in Ligga and Axia approved a new redemption of Class C preferred shares. Meanwhile, JBS (+0.5%) requested cancellation of its public-company registration.

Silver Slips on Hawkish Fed Signals — Silver fell below $66 an ounce on Tuesday, remaining under pressure as hawkish remarks from Federal Reserve officials reinforced expectations that US interest rates will stay higher for longer. The US central bank raised its policy rate by 25 basis points last week, its first such move in three years, while Chair Kevin Warsh signaled that further rate increases could come in the months ahead. Other Fed officials, including St. Louis Fed President Alberto Musalem and Chicago Fed President Austan Goolsbee, continued to stress the need for additional tightening to contain inflation fueled by strong demand and rising energy prices. Markets are now pricing in roughly a 90% chance of another rate hike in December, according to the CME FedWatch Tool. Meanwhile, oil prices fell for a fifth consecutive session following reports that Iran could reopen the Strait of Hormuz within seven days if the US takes initial steps to ease military pressure.

Baltic Dry Index at Over 1-Week High — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, advanced for a fourth straight session on Tuesday, rising about 1% to its highest since September 14 at 3,432 points. The panamax index, which usually carries 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, increased by 1.9% to 2,299 points; and the capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes, including iron ore and coal, went up by 0.9% to 5,892 points. Among smaller vessels, the supramax index rose by 0.3% to 1,776 points.

US Stocks Little Changed — US stock indices were muted on Tuesday, holding on to yesterday’s gains, supported by lower energy prices and optimism over revenue growth from AI agents. The S&P 500 and Dow inched higher to hover less than 0.5% away from record highs touched last month, and the Dow booked small gains. Stocks were broadly higher as oil and fuel prices softened for a fifth session following signs that Iran, the GCC, and the US could chase diplomacy to restore some oil trade in the Persian Gulf, trimming the surge in bond yields this month. AI hyperscalers, which have issued record levels of corporate debt despite high borrowing costs, were higher with Microsoft, Oracle, and Alphabet adding 1%. Banks and industrials also gained. ### 2026-09-22 21:25:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Nigeria #Interest Rate #Importance 2

Nigeria Delivers Massive Rate Cut — The Central Bank of Nigeria slashed its key interest rate by 350 bps to 23% during its September 2026 meeting, its biggest reduction since 2007, after leaving rates unchanged in May and July. Policymakers said the move was intended to restore the effectiveness of monetary policy transmission, as market rates had increasingly diverged from the benchmark lending rate. The Committee assessed that the current macroeconomic environment remains supportive of such an adjustment without undermining the disinflation process. The annual inflation rate eased marginally to 15.39% in August 2026 from 15.43% in July, marking the lowest since March and the third straight month of slowdown. Governor Olayemi Cardoso said inflation was expected to moderate further in the short to medium term, with previous tightening, exchange-rate stability and improved inflation expectations helping to sustain the trend.

【Meta帶火AI助手，但新產品已被亞馬遜封禁】Meta的最新AI代理Muse，正在海外成為現象級產品。第三方市場研究機構Apptopia資料顯示，Muse移動端目前的下載量，已經超過ChatGPT同期的下載量。上線12天內，Muse的全球安裝量達到280萬次，並迅速躍居美國蘋果應用商店第一名。這款9月8日上線的產品，本質上是一款可以替使用者“動手”的AI助手，核心能力是自主執行任務，包括訂票、處理郵件、呼叫Meta旗下社交媒體等。9月21日當天，Meta股價大漲11.43%，達到741.24美元，創一年多以來的最大單日漲幅，同時帶動與AI業務密切相關的半導體板塊上漲。這種增長速度被視為AI代理成為主流的訊號。據媒體報道，OpenAI也在加速開發面向消費者的AI代理，期望與Meta的Muse、馬斯克旗下的Grok Bot展開競爭。但在AI代理獲得消費市場青睞的同時，它與網際網路平臺的衝突也暴露出來。9月20日，亞馬遜封禁Muse的操作，使用者讓Muse在亞馬遜下單時會收到警告：“未經授權的AI代理違反亞馬遜使用條款。”（21財經）

【2026年全國檢驗檢測機構開放日活動在蘇州啟動】9月22日，以“檢驗檢測——賦能高質量發展”為主題的2026年全國檢驗檢測機構開放日主會場活動在江蘇省蘇州市正式啟動。同期，全國共有2663家檢驗檢測機構對外開放，其中603家為年度首次開放，154家國家質檢中心參與，預計受眾超10萬人次。本屆開放日的主會場活動，配套檢驗檢測行業與高階儀器儀表產業高質量發展展示，100家知名機構及企業集中亮相，覆蓋新能源、半導體、智慧網聯汽車、光伏儲能、生物醫藥、機器人等新興產業。20多個省（區、市）市場監管部門及轄區機構同步連線觀看主會場活動，遼寧、安徽、山東、重慶、四川與主會場影片連線開放現場情況。各地還將同步開展公益檢測與便民諮詢，推動檢驗檢測服務貼近產業與民生。檢驗檢測是重要的生產性服務業和高技術服務業，市場監管總局將2026年確定為認證認可檢驗檢測“規範與發展年”，全面實施“一單一庫”管理，印發《國家質檢中心提質最佳化三年行動方案》，引導行業從“事後把關”向“全程賦能”轉型。在此背景下，檢驗檢測機構正加速嵌入產業鏈供應鏈，從單一檢測向全鏈條技術解決方案升級；同時築牢公信力生命線，以“零容忍”整治違規，推動實驗室在陽光下執行。行業也正積極推動檢測國際互認，讓“中國檢測”成為跨國信任通用語言，並深化食品、環境、公共安全等領域的常態化惠民服務。

### 2026-09-22 20:34:38  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Sugar Futures Remain Above 18 Cents — US sugar futures traded above 18 cents per pound, holding close to their highest since April 2025, amid lingering concerns over the supply outlook. Weather disruptions in Asia and Europe are tightening the global outlook, with recent estimates pointing to a potential deficit in 2026/27. Thai sugar production is expected to fall by at least 17% to below 10 million tonnes, while below-normal monsoon rainfall in India is adding to concerns over output. The European Union Sugar Market Observatory also reported recently that the bloc's production in the 2026/27 crop year is expected to fall by 19%. El Niño poses an additional risk to global sugar production, with a strong event potentially bringing drier conditions to major producers. In top producer Brazil, uncertainty centers on how weather will affect the key Center-South region and the split of sugarcane between sugar and ethanol.

【Ukraine drone strikes close Black Sea routes; Russia to export southern grain via Murmansk from next month using fertilizer-loading gear】Ukrainian drone attacks have left Black Sea shipping routes effectively closed, prompting Russia to begin routing grain from its south through the Arctic port of Murmansk as soon as next month. Murmansk will use equipment normally employed for fertilizer loading and a direct-transfer system that loads railcars straight into ship holds—technology typically used for potash—and is upgrading conveyor belts to handle larger grain volumes. The port is roughly 2,700 km from Russia’s main grain gateway Novorossiysk but can be accessed via neutral waters. Russian firms are converting fertilizer, coal and other bulk terminals in Baltic and Arctic ports, including Murmansk, to process grain exports.

### 2026-09-22 20:22:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mexico #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Mexico Retail Sales Growth Slows in July — Mexico’s retail sales rose 1.8% year over year in July 2026, easing from a 2.7% increase in June. Growth was led by sales of healthcare articles (8.7%), followed by household appliances, computers, home décor and second-hand goods (8.4%), hardware, paint and glass products (6.6%), stationery, recreational goods and other personal-use items (3.1%), and textiles, accessories, footwear and clothing (2%). These gains more than offset declines in sales through online channels, printed catalogs, TV and similar platforms (-7.7%), breaking a multi-month streak of gains, as well as grocery items, food, beverages, ice and tobacco (-2.3%) and supermarkets and department stores (-0.6%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail sales edged 0.1% lower, following a 0.2% decline in June.

Hungary Holds Rate as Expected — The National Bank of Hungary left its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 5.5% at its September 2026 meeting, as expected. The decision followed four rate cuts this year and came amid elevated global risks, with geopolitical tensions continuing to pressure energy prices. Headline inflation edged up to 1.3% in August from 1.2% in the previous month, remaining below the central bank’s 2%-4% target range, while gains in the forint helped lower import costs and improve the inflation outlook. Meanwhile, the forint appreciated 5.8% against the euro this year, supported by a pledge from Prime Minister Magyar to cut the budget deficit and to put the country on a path toward adopting the euro.

【SAMR and MEE offices launch baseline review of emissions measurement at key emitters in China’s national carbon market】The general offices of the State Administration for Market Regulation (SAMR) and the Ministry of Ecology and Environment (MEE) issued a joint notice launching a baseline carbon emissions measurement review of key emitters covered by China’s national carbon market. The exercise, aimed at implementing Party Central Committee and State Council directives, is intended to provide a clear roadmap for emitters’ carbon measurement management and to strengthen data safeguards for fair trading. Assessments will cover firms’ carbon measurement management systems, allocation, management and use of measurement instruments, and digital/intelligent upgrades, with the objective of mapping current practices and identifying weak points.

【報道：韓國選定得州電廠作為首個美國投資專案】議會訊息人士週二透露，韓國選定在美國得克薩斯州建設一座大型燃氣發電廠，作為其承諾向美國投入3500億美元投資框架下的首個落地專案。訊息人士稱，經過數月談判落實韓美聯合情況說明書（JFS），韓國產業通商資源部在閉門會議上向國會貿易委員會彙報了該方案。這份聯合檔案以韓國承諾赴美投資，換取美國下調進口關稅。據介紹，韓國產業部擬定的第二、第三項投資計劃，分別是在美國建設8座大型核反應堆，以及阿拉斯加液化天然氣專案。委員會官員表示，這座得州電廠選址恩西納爾市，總裝機容量6.3吉瓦。當前得州大量新建半導體工廠與AI資料中心，電力需求持續攀升。該專案投資額223億美元。韓國政府預估，未來20年專案最高可創造454億美元收入。與首個專案不同，第二、第三項專案仍需與美方進一步磋商。有報道稱，韓方希望收購美國能源企業西屋電氣約20%股份，用於建設8座核反應堆，但美方對此持反對態度。

【四川力爭2030年核產業規模突破2000億元】9月22日，在四川省綿陽市舉行的2026核技術應用暨核醫療產業創新發展大會上，四川省釋出涉及核科技與產業高質量發展、可控核聚變產業中長期發展規劃、核醫療產業發展“十五五”規劃等一系列檔案，其中提出到2030年全省核產業規模突破2000億元。四川是核工業與核技術利用大省，目前已構建起“核素—核藥—核醫療裝備—臨床應用”全產業鏈。“我們探索了‘創新平臺遴選轉化+科創投公司投早投小’成果轉化模式，已在綿陽轉化核醫療科研成果52項、實現投產27項，7個產品獲批國省首臺套（首批次）產品。”綿陽科技城管委會副主任韓曉清說。系列檔案提出，到2030年，四川將培育2家以上百億級龍頭企業，核醫療產業直接經濟產值突破300億元；圍繞核聚變能，加快關鍵核心技術攻關和非發電場景示範應用，建設國內一流的可控核聚變產業聚集區。（新華社）

### 2026-09-22 19:42:33  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy

【European gas falls to September low on hopes of eased Strait of Hormuz tensions】Dutch TTF and UK NBP wholesale gas prices fell more than 6% to three-week lows on Tuesday. The move followed Iran saying it could quickly reopen the Strait of Hormuz if the United States eases military pressure. Prices had already retreated on Monday amid renewed diplomatic expectations, leaving a roughly 12% week-to-date decline. Rasmussen, chief analyst at a global risk firm, said markets are focused on an upcoming UN meeting and the prospect of US‑Iran talks, but added direct talks are unconfirmed and any deal would likely require substantial US concessions that may be politically difficult ahead of the election. A trader said weather and Asian demand will be the key drivers over the next month, with the threshold for demand disruption likely to determine price direction.

【李強在上海調研時強調 加快培育壯大新興產業和未來產業 持續做大做強先進製造業】中共中央政治局常委、國務院總理李強9月22日在上海調研。他強調，要深入貫徹落實習近平總書記關於發展先進製造業的重要指示和全國先進製造業大會精神，堅持智慧化、綠色化、融合化方向，推進科技創新和產業創新深度融合，大力發展新一代智慧製造，加快培育壯大新興產業和未來產業，持續做大做強先進製造業。

在上海電氣國家人工智慧應用中試基地，李強察看專用機器人、工業機器人產品及其在複雜場景中的應用展示，聽取“人工智慧+”整合服務商、人工智慧原生工廠建設情況彙報，參觀智慧機器人裝配除錯中試車間。李強指出，人工智慧等新興產業發展日新月異，相關技術應用正在深刻重塑製造業技術體系、生產模式和產業形態，為製造業科技創新與品牌提升開啟了全新空間。要將我國製造業和數智技術兩方面優勢結合起來，縱深推進“人工智慧+製造”，推動人工智慧更好賦能先進製造業發展，促進研發、生產、管理等全流程智慧升級，進一步啟用資料要素潛能，帶動更多行業全面數智化轉型，打造更加智慧的產業鏈供應鏈。要瞄準製造領域共性難題，圍繞工業設計、中試驗證、智慧運維等關鍵環節，打造更多製造業生產服務平臺，強化整合服務和多樣化解決方案供給，助力各類企業特別是中小企業更加高效、低成本開展智慧化改造，不斷增強發展動力和競爭優勢。

上海未來啟點社群是一個專注於未來產業領域的科創孵化平臺。李強來到這裡，詳細瞭解社群創新生態建設、上海未來產業基金執行等情況，察看腦機介面、量子計算等領域技術產品，與科創人員、社群工作人員親切交流。李強指出，未來產業是先進製造業發展的潛在藍海。要加強對未來技術發展的跟蹤分析和研判，強化關鍵核心技術攻關，築牢基礎研究支撐，鼓勵大膽探索、包容試錯，不斷拓展產業創新前沿。要構建一流創新生態，推動產學研用深度融合，做強科技服務，積極培育和開放應用場景。針對未來產業成長週期長、市場風險大的特點，要大力發展創業投資，壯大耐心資本，建立未來產業投入增長和風險分擔機制。李強強調，人才是第一資源，擁有人才才能贏得未來。要強化科研機構、創新平臺、企業、科技計劃的人才集聚培養功能，探索拔尖創新人才培養新模式，努力造就更多高素質人才。（新華社）

US Futures Hold Near Records — US equity futures were muted on Tuesday, holding gains from the previous session amid softer borrowing costs for the corporate sector and new optimism on returns on AI agents. Contracts for the S&P 500, Dow, and Nasdaq 100 were flat, with the former two hovering less than 0.5% from their record highs. Stocks were broadly higher premarket as oil and fuel prices softened for a fifth session following signs that Iran, the GCC, and the US could chase diplomacy to restore some oil trade in the Persian Gulf, trimming the surge in bond yields this month. AI hyperscalers, which have issued record levels of corporate debt despite high borrowing costs, were higher with Microsoft, Oracle, and Alphabet adding 1%. Banks and industrials also gained. In turn, chip producers inched down but still held most of their surge yesterday, buoyed by positive responses from Meta's Muse AI agent. AMD, Micron, and Intel inched down 1%.

【Iran signals possible Strait of Hormuz reopening; euro-area bond yields fall】Iran signaled it could reopen the Strait of Hormuz if the US eases military pressure, prompting oil prices to fall and pushing euro-area bond yields lower for a second session to near two-week lows. Germany's 10-year bund yield, the euro-area benchmark, briefly rose 4bps at the open then fell 1bp to 3.441%. On Monday it had dropped 7bps to its lowest level since Sep 10. Yields had risen in recent weeks as markets priced further central bank tightening to counter energy-driven inflation; traders now price about 35bps more ECB tightening this year, down from 40bps expected on Friday. Rabobank senior rates strategist Lyn Graham-Taylor said Iran comments drove oil down, weighing on yields.

Sensex Finishes Lower — India’s BSE Sensex closed about 0.4% down at 74,529.1 on Tuesday, after a choppy session, reversing the prior's day advance. Weakness in financials and tech stocks weighed, despite some relief from the pullback in oil prices. Meanwhile, investors were watching for possible diplomatic engagement between the US and Iran at the United Nations General Assembly this week. US President Trump is set to speak later today and said he was open to meeting Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian. Traders also eyed the National Stock Exchange IPO, which drew over $10 billion in bids and was subscribed 5.71 times, with shares set to debut on Thursday. Bajaj Finserv (-1.6%), Bajaj Finance (-1.5%), Trent (-1.5%), Sun Pharma (-1.3%) and TCS (-1.1%) were the top losers. Infosys (-1%), HCL Tech (-0.8%) and Tech Mahindra (-0.5%) came under pressure as CLSA and Goldman Sachs pointed to weak demand weighing on the medium-term outlook. On the flip side, Eternal (1.9%) and IndiGo (1.6%) posted the biggest gains.

### 2026-09-22 18:55:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 2

Treasury Yields Move Lower — The US 10-year Treasury yield fell nearly 2bps to 4.93% on Tuesday, reversing a modest early-session rise, as oil prices declined for a fifth straight session. Renewed hopes for a diplomatic solution to the conflict with Iran pushed oil down and helped ease concerns over inflationary pressures. Treasury yields have generally declined since the Fed raised rates last week for the first time since 2023, while Chair Warsh reaffirmed the central bank’s commitment to bring price stability, helping to restore its credibility. The decline in borrowing costs comes despite the Fed’s more hawkish tone, reinforced by recent comments from policymakers. Chicago Fed President Austan Goolsbee said the central bank cannot overlook persistent supply shocks, while St. Louis Fed President Alberto Musalem said further rate increases may be needed to bring inflation back toward target. Markets currently expect at least one more rate hike this year.

【英偉達市盈率降至十多年來最低 獲利力能否持久或遭市場質疑】英偉達的股票估值不斷下跌，正對這家晶片製造商能否維持其爆發性獲利增長的前景發出警告訊號。根據資料，英偉達目前的股價對應未來12個月預估獲利的市盈率已低於17倍，處於十多年來的最低便宜水平。這僅為2025年時的一半(當時英偉達的營收與獲利增速較慢)，且大幅低於今年5月創下的25倍以上預估市盈率。TCW主題股票和可持續增長股票高階投資組合經理Eli Horton表示，這表明市場對該公司目前獲利能力的永續性抱持著健康的懷疑態度。鑑於英偉達令人驚歎的基本面背景，其股價表現令人驚訝，但這也告訴你，市場預期低於目前的普遍共識預估。儘管英偉達股價在週一結束了連續五天的漲勢，但其折價估值依然存在。在此之前，半導體股出現了更廣泛的反彈。AI業領袖們稍早呼籲放慢最先進AI模型開發速度的言論嚇壞了投資人，導致費城半導體指數(SOX)在9月14日下跌近6%。

Ireland Wholesale Inflation Eases in August — Wholesale prices in Ireland rose 6.5% year-on-year in August 2026, easing from a 7.3% increase in July which was the highest since October 2022. Domestic prices increased 2.4%, while export producer prices rose 6.9%. Producer prices recorded notable year-on-year increases in chemicals and chemical products (16.5%), electrical equipment (6.1%), other non-metallic mineral products (6.0%), fabricated metal products excluding machinery and equipment (4.9%), and basic metals (4.8%). Food products rose 0.6%, driven by fish and fish products (8.6%) and other food products (5.8%), while meat and meat products fell 9.6%. Meanwhile, energy fuels surged 34.5%, electricity prices jumped 76.9%, and construction products rose 3.3%. On a monthly basis, wholesale prices fell 0.8% in August, reversing a 3.2% increase in July.

### 2026-09-22 18:09:08  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Factory Orders #Importance 2

UK Factory Orders Highest Since July 2023 — The UK’s total order book balance improved to -9 in September 2026 from -25 in August, marking the strongest reading since July 2023 and beating market expectations of -34. Export orders also improved, while remaining below “normal” at -9, but well above the historical average of -19. Output fell only marginally, with the balance improving sharply to -4 from -17. Manufacturers expect output to decline slightly again through December, at -6. Selling-price expectations also eased considerably to +12 from +22 in August, moving closer to the long-run average of +8 and suggesting some cooling in cost pressures. However, finished-goods inventories were only just adequate, with the balance falling to +2. The CBI said the data point to tentative stabilisation in manufacturing, but warned that high energy, employment and supply-chain costs continue to constrain firms. It said the upcoming Budget could help support investment and growth by reducing business costs and improving confidence.

### 2026-09-22 18:08:57  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Steel Declines from 2-Week High — Steel rebar futures fell to CNY 3,100 per tonne from the two-week high of CNY 3,130 on September 16th amid lower iron ore prices and downside risks on demand. The property crisis in top steel consumer China continued to prevent significant demand growth for rebar. Housing prices fell 3% annually in August as existing home sales took the majority of home sales in the country on a year-to-date basis, reflecting the government's effort to cap the oversupply of residential properties. The developments limit steel buying from major developers in the country, among the main buyers of rebar in the world. Consistently, the official construction PMI in China feel to a record low of 46.9. Elsewhere, a group of trade finance firms accused trader Radiant World of fraudulent invoices to back receivables, driving ore prices to fall sharply as trade finance liquidity dried.

【外交部：願同東盟各國落實好自貿區3.0版】第23屆中國—東盟博覽會21日成功閉幕，外交部發言人郭嘉昆22日表示，中方願同東盟各國落實好自貿區3.0版，攜手共建更為緊密的中國—東盟命運共同體。當日例行記者會上，有記者問：昨天，第23屆中國—東盟博覽會成功閉幕，取得不少新成果。上週，平陸運河正式通航受到國內外輿論高度關注。有評論稱，東博會“黃金平臺”和平陸運河“黃金通道”將開啟中國—東盟合作新篇章。發言人對此有何評論？郭嘉昆說，中國—東盟博覽會是雙方合作的“金色名片”。23年來，東博會見證中國與東盟從“黃金十年”到“鑽石十年”再到自貿區3.0版的升級跨越。本屆東博會首次設立“東盟國家需求展區”，首次釋出東盟國家需求清單，以“東盟出題、中國解題”新模式，推動供需精準對接。“本屆東博會還專門設立了平陸運河展區，運河通航後將大幅縮短中國西南地區到東盟國家的海運距離，開闢雙方貿易往來新通道。”郭嘉昆表示，“中方願同東盟各國落實好自貿區3.0版，攜手共建更為緊密的中國—東盟命運共同體。”（新華社）

Cotton Falls to One-Week Low — Cotton futures fell to around 82 cents per pound, its lowest level in little over a week, as traders assess whether demand can keep pace with the expected rise in new-crop availability. As the seasonal harvest progresses, additional supply is weighing on prices, particularly in Arizona, where 39% of the crop had been harvested, and Texas, up to 23%, in the week ending September 20th. Additionally, while high temperatures and lack of precipitation, particularly in Texas, increasing stress on crops, its conditions continue to worsen, with only 34% of crops rated good or excelent on the same week. Bearish sentiment was reinforced by weak export demand, as USDA's latest export sales report showed shipments declined to 71.2 thousand running bales for the week ended September 10th. Meanwhile, Brazilian output for 2025/26 is estimated to reach a record 4.5 million tons of lint, according to Agroconsult, while exports of Brazilian cotton surged 37.3% year-on-year in August.

Euro Falls as Markets Weigh Middle East Risks — The euro weakened to $1.145, its lowest level since late July, as investors assessed developments in the Middle East while the USD remained supported by expectations of further Federal Reserve rate hikes. Brent crude resumed its decline after reports that Iran had offered to reopen the Strait of Hormuz within seven days if Washington lifts its military blockade of Iranian ports. Meanwhile, ECB’s Chief Economist Philip Lane warned that a new wave of higher energy prices could keep Eurozone inflation elevated for longer than policymakers initially anticipated. He said the shock should result in higher and more persistent inflation before a decline toward the ECB’s target from mid-2027. Following this month’s second rate hike since the Iran war sent energy costs soaring, ECB officials have begun laying the groundwork for further tightening. Eurozone inflation is expected to reach around 4% in coming months, with the ECB projecting 3% this year and 2.5% next year, well above its 2% target.

European Natural Gas Prices Fall to 3-Week Low — European natural gas prices fell toward €70 per megawatt-hour, their lowest level in three weeks, after reports that Iran could reopen the Strait of Hormuz if the US lifted its blockade of Iranian ports. Japan’s Kyodo News reported that Tehran had proposed reopening the waterway within seven days, potentially providing a basis for diplomatic discussions at the UN General Assembly. President Donald Trump is due to address the assembly and may meet Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian. Rising numbers of LNG tankers and crude carriers using alternative routes have also eased supply concerns, while LNG demand in Northeast Asia remains weak, particularly in China, Japan and Turkey. In Europe, warmer and drier weather is expected over the next two weeks. Gas storage stood at 69.94% full, up 0.31 percentage points day on day but 11.76 points below last year. Meanwhile, Norwegian gas exports remained constrained by maintenance, with bookings falling to 262.5 million cubic metres per day.

UK Gilt Yields Fall as Oil Slides Below $100 — UK 10-year gilt yields reversed an early rise to fall to 5.20% on Tuesday, their lowest level since September 8, extending an eight-basis-point decline in the previous session as oil prices resumed their recent slide. Brent crude fell below $100 a barrel following reports that Iran could reopen the Strait of Hormuz if the US lifted its blockade of Iranian ports. Meanwhile, UK government data showed the budget deficit widened to £18.3 billion in August, above economists’ expectations of £15.5 billion. The larger shortfall was partly attributed to inflation, which has increased spending on state pensions and social benefits. Attention is now turning to next month’s Autumn Budget, with the government reportedly considering changes to fiscal rules that would allow borrowing for infrastructure and other construction projects to be excluded. A similar proposal was previously floated during Keir Starmer’s time in office.

Luxembourg Current Account Deficit Narrows in Q2 — Luxembourg’s current account deficit narrowed sharply to EUR 436.5 million in the second quarter of 2026 from EUR 1,482.8 million in the corresponding quarter of the previous year. The goods account surplus widened to EUR 327.7 million from EUR 152.8 million, while the services account surplus also increased to EUR 7,684.2 million from EUR 5,172.0 million in Q2 2025. On the other hand, the primary income deficit expanded to EUR 8,452.8 million from EUR 6,793.7 million, driven largely by wider deficits in both employee income (EUR 3,828.5 million vs EUR 3,676.7 million) and investment income (EUR 4,624.2 million vs EUR 3,116.9 million), while the secondary income deficit grew to EUR 20.1 million from EUR 13.9 million.

Croatia Jobless Rate Steady at 3.4% — Croatia’s registered unemployment rate remained unchanged at 3.4% from the previous month. The number of unemployed persons increased 1.8% month-on-month to 63.2 thousand, while total employment edged up 0.6% to 1.777 million. The labor force also increased 0.7% to 1.840 million. Meanwhile, paid employment in legal entities rose 0.8% to 1.520 million. Conversely, employment in crafts, trades, and freelance activities fell 0.3% to 240.2 thousand, while employment among private farmers dropped 0.4% to 17.3 thousand. Compared to August 2025, the labor market remained weaker overall, with the labor force down 1.7% and total employment declining 1.1%, while paid employment in legal entities fell 1.5%. Over the same period, the registered unemployment fell further by 16.3%.

### 2026-09-22 17:34:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #Morocco #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Morocco Posts Deflation for 2nd Month — Consumer prices in Morocco fell by 0.3% year-on-year in August 2026, following a 0.6% decrease in July, as deflation persisted for a second straight month. Food and non-alcoholic beverages posted a sharper price decline of 4.2% in August, compared with 4.0% in July, while communication prices fell 0.2% for a second month. Meanwhile, inflation moderated for housing and utilities (1.4% vs 1.5%) and restaurants and hotels (2.3% vs 2.4%). On the other hand, price growth accelerated sharply for transportation (7.3% vs 4.1%). The core inflation index, which excludes products with volatile prices and those subject to public tariffs, increased by 0.2% from July and by 0.1% from August 2025. On a monthly basis, the CPI rose by 0.8%, after a 1% decline in the previous month.

Wheat Falls as US Weather Improves, Black Sea Risks Persist — Wheat fell to around $7.2 per bushel, moving further below the 3-1/2-year high of $7.67 reached on August 28, as improving weather in parts of the US Plains eased concerns over winter wheat conditions. The USDA said recent rainfall across the northern and central Plains had improved topsoil moisture and prospects for germination, although planting remained behind average at 17% complete versus the five-year average of 21%. Kansas was 10% planted, Oklahoma 4% and Texas 21%. Meanwhile, continued attacks between Russia and Ukraine have clouded prospects for a recovery in Black Sea grain exports, with strikes on vessels and ports leaving seaborne shipments largely disrupted. Russia has increasingly redirected grain through Baltic and Arctic terminals, although volumes remain below normal Black Sea flows. Markets are also monitoring diplomatic efforts at the UN and the Trump-Xi meeting for potential developments affecting energy flows and Chinese agricultural purchases.

Rubber Futures Hold in Tight Range — Rubber futures held around 238 US cents per kilogram in late September, moving within a tight range after retreating from a 13-year high reached earlier this month. Immediate supply risks eased somewhat as rainfall in parts of Thailand was expected to improve, potentially supporting rubber tapping, although El Niño-related dryness remains a concern across much of Southeast Asia. Meanwhile, softer demand from China, the world's largest rubber consumer, also weighed on prices as weaker tire production and operating rates reduced raw-rubber purchases. Some manufacturers also plan holiday closures ahead of China's National Day and Mid-Autumn Festival, which could further reduce near-term rubber demand. In the meantime, crude oil prices decreased for the fifth day, making synthetic rubber cheaper and reducing the relative appeal of natural rubber.

Slovenia Jobless Rate Edges Up in July — Slovenia’s unemployment rate edged up to 4.4% in July 2026 from 4.3% in the previous month. The number of unemployed people increased by 1,012 from the previous month to 43,257, while declining by 542 year-on-year. Unemployment among those aged over 55 accounted for the largest share of unemployment (24.2%), followed by those aged 40–49 (23.9%) and those aged 30–39 (21.4%). Meanwhile, the share of unemployed people with tertiary education rose to 19.3% from 18.5% in the previous month, while first-time jobseekers accounted for 16.6%, down from 17.7%. Palm Oil Holds at Elevated Level — Malaysian palm oil futures hovered above MYR 4,850 per tonne, holding their jump since mid-August on strong demand for vegetable oils. Biofuel feedstock costs saw a broad increase from palm oil and soy oil, with persistent risks of an escalation in the Middle East and concerns of lower crude oil output from Saudi Arabia lifting demand for biofuel alternatives. On top of that, Indonesia’s B50 biodiesel mandate is set to divert more palm oil to domestic use. Likewise, imports from top consumer India rose 7% from July to 782,761 tonnes in August, the highest since February, as refiners replenished stocks ahead of the festival season. In turn, the forecast of one of the most aggressive El Ninos in history continued to threaten supply from major producers Malaysia and Indonesia.

### 2026-09-22 16:21:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #Spain #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Spain Trade Deficit Deepens in July — Spain’s trade deficit widened to €5.22 billion in July 2026 from €4.01 billion in the same period a year earlier. Imports rose 5.9% year-on-year to €40.68 billion, driven by higher purchases of vehicles and motorcycles (19.8%), energy products (13.4%), namely oil and derivatives (9.9%), electronic equipment (22.7%), and minerals (45.8%). In contrast, purchases of chemical products fell 2.8%. Among key trading partners, imports rose from China (9.9%), Algeria (61.7%), Italy (8.7%), and the UK (26.4%). Meanwhile, exports rose at a slower pace of 3% to €35.45 billion, supported by higher shipments of energy products (60.8%), particularly oil and derivatives (62.8%). Shipments of general-use equipment increased 16.5%, while exports of non-ferrous metals and minerals rose 22.2% and 26.2%, respectively. By destination, shipments were mainly directed to eurozone countries, particularly Italy (14.4%), Portugal (8.4%), and Germany (5.6%), as well as the US (19.2%).

Taiwan Export Orders Hit Fresh Record High — Orders for Taiwanese exports surged 71.4% year-on-year to a new record high of USD 103 billion in August 2026, following a 61.9% gain in the previous month. The latest figure surpassed $100 billion for the first time, as robust AI demand continued to fuel expansion for the world’s leading supplier of AI chips and technology. Strong growth was recorded in information and communication products (100.5% vs 89.5% in July), basic metals and articles (26.8% vs 24.3%), electronic products (83.9% vs 71.7%), and mineral products (91.6% vs 44.4%). Moreover, export orders for optical, photographic and cinematographic apparatus declined at a softer pace (-5% vs -10.6%). On the other hand, growth slowed for machinery (28.3% vs 31.3%) and electrical machinery products (14.7% vs 16.7%), while orders for transport equipment slipped (-0.1% vs 6.4%). Among major markets, export order growth remained strong, led by the US (88.9%), ASEAN countries (70.5%), and Mainland China and Hong Kong (47.7%).

Meanwhile, UK government data showed the budget deficit widened to £18.3 billion in August, above economists’ expectations of £15.5 billion. The wider shortfall was partly attributed to the impact of inflation, which has increased government spending on items including state pensions and social benefits. Attention is now turning to next month’s Autumn Budget, with the government reportedly considering changes to its fiscal rules that would allow borrowing for infrastructure and other building projects to be excluded. A similar proposal was previously floated during Keir Starmer’s time in office.

### 2026-09-22 15:28:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sri Lanka #Credit Rating #Importance 0

Fitch Upgrades Sri Lanka's Credit Rating to B- — Fitch Credit Ratings upgraded Sri Lanka's long-term foreign currency credit rating to ´B-' from 'CCC+' on September 22, 2026, citing progress in macroeconomic stabilization and structural reforms that have reduced external financing risks. The agency highlighted improved fiscal and external balances and a modest rebuilding of FX reserves. Fitch expects fiscal discipline and revenue mobilization to support sustained primary surpluses, helping keep government debt on a downward path and reducing the risk of renewed external imbalances. Primary surpluses are expected to remain above 2% of GDP, with government debt falling to 92.9% of GDP in 2026 from 96.7% in 2025. However, Fitch noted Sri Lanka's credit profile remains constrained by still-high government debt and debt-servicing ratios compared with rating peers, with external debt service set to increase over the next five years. S&P’s credit rating stands at CCC+, while Moody’s rating is Caa1, both with a stable outlook.

### 2026-09-22 15:24:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #Switzerland #Current Account #Importance 1

Switzerland Current Account Surplus Hits 1-Year High — Switzerland’s current account surplus widened to CHF 23.7 billion in the second quarter of 2026 from a downwardly revised CHF 10.2 billion in the corresponding quarter of the previous year. This marked the largest surplus since the first quarter of 2025, as the goods account surplus increased to CHF 29.7 billion from CHF 17.1 billion, while the primary income account swung to a surplus of CHF 0.3 billion from a deficit of CHF 1.7 billion, largely driven by higher investment income of CHF 8.3 billion from CHF 6.5 billion, while the labour income deficit remained unchanged at CHF 8.2 billion. Additionally, the secondary income deficit narrowed to CHF 1.7 billion from CHF 2.9 billion a year earlier. On the other hand, the services account deficit widened to CHF 4.4 billion from CHF 2.2 billion.

Turkey Consumer Confidence at Over 8-Year High — Turkey’s consumer confidence index rose to 91.9 in September 2026 from 90.8 in the previous month. It marked the highest reading since July 2018, as expectations for households’ financial situation over the next 12 months improved to 93.7 from 93.1, while confidence in the general economic outlook rose to 89.8 from 89.4. Consumers also showed a stronger willingness to make major purchases, with the index measuring intentions to spend on durable goods climbing to 108.8 from 105.1. On the other hand, assessments of current household finances edged down to 75.3 from 75.4. Despite the improvement in sentiment, the headline index remained below the neutral 100 mark, suggesting that consumers continued to hold a broadly pessimistic view of economic conditions.

### 2026-09-22 15:09:56  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Leading Economic Index #Importance 1

South Africa Leading Index Drops 0.9% MoM in July — South Africa’s composite leading business cycle indicator fell by 0.9% month-over-month in July 2026, following a revised 1.0% decline in June. Six of the 10 available component time series decreased, outweighing gains in the remaining four. The largest negative contributions came from a slowdown in the six-month smoothed growth rate of real M1 money supply and a decline in residential building plans approved. Meanwhile, the biggest positive contributions came from an acceleration in the six-month smoothed growth rate of job advertisements and a widening interest rate spread. The coincident business cycle indicator edged down by 0.1% in June, mainly due to lower real wholesale, retail and motor trade sales, while the lagging indicator rose by 0.2%.

【國家衛生健康委釋出50項新食品安全國家標準和修改單】根據《食品安全法》規定，國家衛生健康委、市場監管總局聯合印發2026年第6號公告，釋出50項新食品安全國家標準和修改單。新發布標準以防風險、保健康、促發展為導向，細化食品安全全鏈條管控要求，既是消費者健康權益的重要保障，也是監管執法的“硬標尺”，更是企業合規生產的重要依據。本次公佈的《食品中丙烯醯胺汙染控制規範》《食品中鉛汙染控制規範》《鍍錫薄鋼板罐裝食品中錫汙染控制規範》等3項食品安全標準是汙染物控制規範類標準，既可以指導企業建立全過程衛生管控體系，根據汙染物來源特點制定不同的管控措施，從生產源頭和過程防範相關汙染風險；又可以為監管部門開展現場監督執法提供依據，服務食品安全全鏈條監管。《牛乳蛋白》立足乳品行業發展實際，明確了牛乳蛋白產品的術語定義、核心技術指標及標籤標識等內容，有助於推動乳成分加工有序規範發展，保障牛乳蛋白產品使用者和消費者的知情權、選擇權。《食品安全國家標準 食品用香料通則》等食品新增劑質量規格標準及修改單規定了食品用香料等食品新增劑的感官指標、主成分含量、雜質要求、鉛、砷等技術指標，增加了商品化產品描述，進一步完善了相關食品新增劑的管理要求。（新華社）

### 2026-09-22 14:56:00  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Nickel Extends Gains on Indonesia Supply Cut — Nickel traded around $16,400 per tonne, extending gains as concerns over supply disruptions in Indonesia emerged. Smelters at the Morowali Industrial Park will cut nickel pig iron production due to an El Niño-driven water shortage, with the affected output potentially reaching around 100,000 tonnes, raising concerns over tighter supply and helping limit further downside. However, the near-term outlook remained constrained by weak Chinese downstream demand, with nickel salt procurement and spot stockpiling subdued while some precursor producers reduced operating rates. Additionally, Indonesia’s revised nickel ore benchmark price formula sharply lowered the HPM for low-grade 1.2% nickel ore to around $24.89 per wet tonne, nearly halving the previous level, which could reduce feedstock costs for HPAL plants and encourage greater use of low-grade reserves. High inventories across the nickel supply chain also pointed to ongoing destocking, limiting the scope for a sustained recovery.

### 2026-09-22 14:55:31  #Trading Economics #Markets #Taiwan #Stock Market #Importance 1

TAIEX Hits Record High at Close — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, advanced 81 points, or 0.2%, to close at 47,800 on Tuesday, gaining for the fifth straight session and hitting a record high, mainly buoyed by gains in electronic technology. The index surpassed the 48,000 mark intraday before pulling back to 47,800 at the close. The broader index tracked a rally on Wall Street overnight, buoyed by solid AI-related stocks, while oil prices and US Treasury yields eased. Optimism over the Trump-Xi summit this week also lifted sentiment, as AI and tariffs are expected to be discussed at the meeting. Electronic technology rose 0.4%, mainly supported by surges in MediaTek (3.5%), ASE Tech (4.5%), and Unimicron Tech (9.8%). By contrast, TSMC, the world's largest contract chipmaker and accounting for more than 40% of the market's total value, fell 0.4%. In the morning session, TSMC rose, fueled by reports that it was in talks to acquire AUO's 5th- and 7.5th-generation fabs in Central Taiwan Science Park.

【凱文·凱利：未來十年AGI不會出現，空間智慧將成AI重要突破方向】9月21日，在長江商學院CEO專案20週年論壇上，美國《連線》雜誌創始主編、《失控》作者凱文·凱利給出約15個關於AI的預測。他認為，至少未來十年不會出現真正意義上的通用人工智慧（AGI）。在他看來，今天的AI正在快速進化，但這種進化並不均勻。“未來也不會只有一個通用AI，而是會出現許多不同型別的AI。”他更傾向於使用複數形式的“AIs”描述未來的人工智慧世界。其它預測還包括，未來五年，空間智慧將成為AI最重要的突破方向之一；未來99%的AI可能都是“隱形”的，它們不會直接與人交流，而是在後臺彼此協作，他尤其看好個人Agent與智慧眼鏡結合；他還認為，真正成熟、普遍可用的人形機器人仍需約十年；AI不會簡單“消滅工作”，而是重組工作；未來兩年，AI醫生會成為一個非常普遍的入口。凱文·凱利還談到了中國AI產業。他認為，中國開源模型發展較快，與美國在最前沿大模型能力上的差距已沒有那麼大。未來五年，中美最前沿模型的能力可能逐漸趨於收斂，但中國模型在成本方面可能具有優勢。在此基礎上，他還提出，未來大約四年，世界上最好的AI晶片可能在中國製造；到2030年，中國有可能製造出全球最好的自動駕駛汽車。

【國際能源署：今年全球煤炭需求將創新高】國際能源署近期釋出的報告預測，今年全球煤炭需求將增長1.2%，達到創紀錄的89.4億噸。國際能源署的這份報告指出，霍爾木茲海峽運輸受阻雖未直接衝擊煤炭供應，卻使液化天然氣運輸量大幅減少，推高天然氣價格，這促使部分具備閒置煤電產能的國家增加燃煤發電。歐洲、日本、韓國等市場的煤炭使用量因此高於此前預期。此外，預計今年出現的強厄爾尼諾現象可能增加印度、越南等國的製冷需求，並減少水力發電量，進一步支撐煤炭消費。需求上升也推動國際煤炭價格走高。今年8月，紐卡斯爾6000千卡動力煤均價為每噸131美元，較2025年均價高出約26%。 （央視財經）

### 2026-09-22 14:38:25  #其他

【《輕工紡織產業發展“十五五”規劃》解讀】《規劃》在提升產業創新能力方面有哪些舉措？

技術創新是輕工紡織產業突破發展瓶頸、重塑競爭優勢的關鍵舉措。《規劃》聚焦破解部分領域核心環節短板、支撐產業由大向強躍遷部署了以下任務：一是加強原始創新和關鍵核心技術攻關。完善“揭榜掛帥”“賽馬”等制度，支援重點企業建立製造業創新中心、企業技術中心等創新平臺，並透過設定專欄明確了19個細分行業關鍵核心技術攻關方向。二是加快科技成果高效轉化應用。提升重點領域中試驗證平臺和產業技術基礎公共服務平臺建設力度，推動檢驗檢測能力和重大儀器裝置共享共用。三是培育優質企業。建立輕工紡織企業競爭力評價指標體系，加強企業競爭力分析研究。培育更多專精特新“小巨人”企業和製造業單項冠軍企業。支援龍頭企業搭建智慧供應鏈平臺，實現大中小企業融通發展。

---

### 2026-09-22 14:38:15  #其他

【《輕工紡織產業發展“十五五”規劃》解讀】《規劃》如何激發產業融合化發展動力？

深化產業融合創新是“十五五”時期輕工紡織產業培育發展新動能的重要途徑。《規劃》從文化賦能、智慧生活、跨界應用等方面提出了融合發展路徑：一是深化文化賦能產業創新。支援各地建設一批傳統工藝傳承與創新基地、工作室、非遺工坊等。開發兼具實用功能和文化創意的產品。推動建立國際絲綢組織。推動樂器製造與音樂教育、音樂生活融合發展。挖掘輕工紡織工業文化價值。在確保安全生產前提下，因地制宜發展工業旅遊。二是培育智慧生活融合業態。實施智慧家居互聯互通行動，加快制定智慧家居互聯互通強制性國家標準。推動智慧服務機器人、智慧眼鏡、智慧玩具、智慧手錶、智慧健身器材、智慧樂器等新一代智慧終端產品創新應用。建設智慧家居體驗中心、智慧社群服務站點等，培育智慧生活新場景。三是推動跨界融合應用。實施“紡織+”行動，推進紡織科學技術與產品在重點領域的深度融合與創新應用，研究制定“紡織+”應用推廣目錄，深化紡織與生物、奈米、仿生等前沿科技的有機融合。圍繞醫療健康、土工建築、海洋工程、電子、新能源等5個重點領域提出融合化應用發展方向。

### 2026-09-22 14:38:08  #其他

【《輕工紡織產業發展“十五五”規劃》解讀】《規劃》如何加快行業智慧化綠色化轉型？

智慧化綠色化轉型是“十五五”時期輕工紡織產業實現質量變革、效率變革的關鍵抓手。《規劃》圍繞數綠協同賦能傳統輕工紡織產業升級部署以下任務：一是推動人工智慧賦能。加快研發重點行業垂直領域大模型，開發應用面向輕工紡織全產業鏈的專用智慧體。分行業梳理AI應用場景和實施路徑，培育一批場景導向數智工具和解決方案，並在14個細分行業部署了人工智慧賦能主要方向。二是加快綠色低碳轉型。引導企業開展產品綠色設計，推廣“智慧+儲能”照明系統、“家電+樓宇”節能系統等。強化環保、能耗、水耗、汙染物排放等強制性國家標準執行。推進陶瓷、日用玻璃、化纖等行業清潔能源替代。制定重點行業綠色低碳技術路線圖，持續提升企業能效碳效水平。三是健全資源迴圈再利用體系。佈局建設廢舊紡織品等資源化利用分類處理中心，推動家電等行業建設再迴圈互聯工廠。有序推進廢舊紡織品等社會化回收渠道和迴圈利用體系建設。鼓勵加大家電、傢俱、塑膠製品等領域再生原料使用比例，推進迴圈再利用生產規範化、標準化發展。

---

### 2026-09-22 14:38:00  #其他

【《輕工紡織產業發展“十五五”規劃》解讀】《規劃》在增強供需適配性方面做哪些具體部署？

供給與消費良性互動、相互促進是輕工紡織產業暢通國內迴圈、釋放內需潛力的關鍵著力點。《規劃》圍繞最佳化供給結構、挖掘細分市場、推動供給與需求更高水平動態平衡，具體部署以下任務：一是提升優質供給能力。釋出適老化產品和服務推廣目錄，編制升級和創新消費品指南。支援產品流行趨勢釋出，開展十大類紡織創新精品建設。同時，聚焦嬰童用品、青少年用品、時尚潮品、老年用品、孕產女性用品、特殊職業/地域人群用品、寵物用品、生產性服務用品等8個重點領域增強供給和需求適配性。二是建設高水平質量標準。研究制定輕工、紡織標準體系建設指南，加強重點領域標準供給。制修訂30項以上強制性國家標準，完成600項左右行業急需標準制修訂。面向輕工紡織重點領域研究制定人工智慧相關標準和認證、標識規範。深入實施製造業卓越質量提升工程。三是強化品牌培育提升。系統培育中國消費名品，推進紡織服裝創意設計領航集聚區、特色產業集聚區建設，分類培育紡織服裝卓越品牌、傳統優勢食品產區和地方特色食品產業。培育專業化品牌服務商，提升輕工紡織品牌的價值創造能力與國際化運營水平。

### 2026-09-22 14:37:52  #其他

【《輕工紡織產業發展“十五五”規劃》解讀】《規劃》部署了哪些重點任務？

《規劃》圍繞保持輕工紡織產業規模優勢、大力提升發展質效目標，部署了14項重點任務和5個專欄。一是提升產業創新能力。包括加強原始創新和關鍵核心技術攻關、加快科技成果高效轉化應用、培育優質企業3項重點任務，以及關鍵核心技術攻關方向專欄。二是增強供給和需求適配性。包括提升優質供給能力、建設高水平質量標準、強化品牌培育提升3項重點任務，以及產品供需適配提升重點領域專欄。三是加快智慧化綠色化轉型。包括推動人工智慧賦能、加快綠色低碳轉型、健全資源迴圈再利用體系3項重點任務，以及人工智慧賦能主要方向、綠色低碳改造升級方向2個專欄。四是拓展產業融合發展路徑。包括深化文化賦能產業創新、培育智慧生活融合業態、推動跨界融合應用3項重點任務，以及融合化應用重點領域專欄。五是統籌最佳化產業佈局。包括強化產業佈局區域協同等2項重點任務。

---

### 2026-09-22 14:37:41  #其他

【《輕工紡織產業發展“十五五”規劃》解讀】二、《規劃》的總體考慮是什麼？

我國輕工紡織產業規模體量已處於全球領先地位。在定位上，《規劃》從過去強調產業大國的規模地位，向更加註重自主創新、改造提升、深度轉型、價值創造等高階發展邏輯轉變。從導向上，《規劃》聚焦融合創新、提質升級、價值創造，明確產業發展思路，凝聚全行業共識，推動企業同步行動、主動轉型，著力建設現代化輕工紡織產業體系。一是強化自主創新。聚焦培育競爭新優勢，加強原始創新和關鍵核心技術攻關，提升科技成果轉化效能，在底層技術上尋求更多突破。二是突出供需適配。主動適應品質化、個性化、服務化消費升級趨勢，豐富優質產品供給，強化品牌培育，加強與文旅健康、銀髮經濟、數字經濟等深度融合，以高質量供給引領和創造新需求。三是堅持價值創造。加快AI技術賦能，推動綠色低碳轉型，大力培育新質生產力，服務國家戰略需要。四是統籌內外佈局。平衡好產業升級、國內產業有序轉移和國際化資源配置的關係。

### 2026-09-22 14:34:59  #Trading Economics #Economy #Denmark #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

Danish Consumer Morale Weakens in September — Denmark’s consumer confidence fell to -13.7 in September 2026 from -13.1 in August, but remained above the six-month average of -15.7. The slight decline reflected consumers’ more unfavorable assessments of their financial situation over the past 12 months (-8.6 vs -7.6 in August), while expectations for household finances over the next year deteriorated (-3.7 vs 0.4). Meanwhile, perceptions of the country’s current economic conditions were slightly less negative (-22.5 vs -22.7), alongside expectations for Denmark’s economy in the year ahead (-19.2 vs -20.6). Consumers’ willingness to make major purchases was also less pessimistic (-14.3 vs -14.9). Among components not included in the overall confidence indicator, perceptions of current price growth eased (28.0 vs 29.9), while inflation expectations for the next 12 months rose (31.9 vs 30.5). Concerns about unemployment also intensified (19.2 vs 15.8), with households continuing to expect joblessness over the next 12 months.

【機構稱韓國散戶失去動能 外資是KOSPI能否反彈的關鍵】一項最新分析顯示，此前支撐韓國股市的散戶資金“資金攻勢”在失去動能，外資買入將成為韓國綜合股價指數（KOSPI）能否反彈的關鍵。若中東戰事緩和、國際油價與美國長期利率企穩，外資資金有望重新迴流韓國本土市場。 Shinyoung Securities研究中心負責人Kim Hak-kyun 22日在首爾汝矣島韓國交易所舉行的新聞簡報會上表示，今年上半年，散戶資金抵消了外資的拋售壓力，但後續市場很難再依靠散戶資金迎來重大轉機。他稱：“外資的買入力度，將決定下半年與上半年截然不同的市場供需格局。”  Kim Hak-kyun結合未交割交易與保證金貸款，對真實資金流向進行測算：8月，散戶資金從韓國股市淨流出13.5萬億韓元（摺合97億美元）。截至本月18日，散戶資金累計流出9.83萬億韓元（摺合71億美元），日均流出規模已經超過8月水平。

European Stocks Set for Positive Open — European equity markets were set to open slightly higher on Tuesday, building on the previous session’s gains after an AI-led rally on Wall Street boosted optimism over semiconductor demand. Market sentiment was also lifted by expectations for a summit between Presidents Donald Trump and Xi Jinping this week, along with hopes for talks between Trump and his Iranian counterpart and other Gulf leaders. At least three refiners received informal assurances from Aramco executives about shipments but have not been given formal notices or a specific timetable. Yanbu loadings were almost entirely halted after the east‑west pipeline was shut on Sept. 10 following drone attacks launched from Iraq; the pipeline had been delivering about 4 mln b/d to the port. A source last week said Saudi Arabia was seeking to restore roughly half the pipeline’s capacity within days, but Aramco has not issued an update.

【羅氏減重注射劑中期試驗資料揭曉 使用者平均體重減輕15.5%】羅氏控股的一項中期試驗資料顯示，對於超重或肥胖的2型糖尿病患者，實驗性減重注射劑Enicepatide降低了血糖和體重。羅氏週二在一份宣告中表示，接受最高劑量24毫克治療的患者，48周後血糖指標HbA1c降低2.65%，平均體重減輕15.5%，且未觀察到平臺期。新試驗中最常見的副作用主要是輕度至中度的胃腸道反應，治療組中只有2.0%的患者中止治療，而安慰劑組的中止治療率為0.0%。“結合持續的體重減輕，Enicepatide有望成為治療2型糖尿病的理想藥物，能夠減少和預防併發症，並改善患者的代謝健康，”首席醫療官兼全球產品開發負責人Levi Garraway表示。

【里昂：看好韓國半導體於Agentic AI供應鏈關鍵地位，予SK海力士“高度確信跑贏大市”評級】里昂發表研報指，代理式AI將大型AI模型的單次推理，轉變為每次任務需執行多輪的長期工具使用迴圈，對儲存器容量、頻寬及機架規模完整性構成壓力，遠超單純運算問題，因而需要高頻寬儲存器(HBM)、低功耗雙倍資料率(LPDDR)/小型壓縮附加儲存器模組(SOCAMM)、企業級固態硬碟(eSSD)/高頻寬快快閃記憶體儲器(HBF)、先進封裝、測試及前端微縮等配合。該行指，韓國在AI儲存器、鍵合、基板、測試及前端處理等關鍵AI半導體工序，擁有廣泛的端到端佈局。該行認為，在結構性Agentic AI需求及供應偏緊支援下，K-enablers基本面改善、定位加強，對SK海力士、三星電機及EO Technics均予“高度確信跑贏大市”評級，對三星電子、韓美半導體、HPSP Eugene及ISC則給予“跑贏大市”評級。

【Goldman Sachs: Huahong Hongli positioned for AI chip demand; Huali Micro buy widens node capability — reiterates Buy, HK$335 PT】Goldman Sachs said it is positive on Huahong Hongli (01347.HK), forecasting QoQ revenue growth of 11% in 3Q2026 and 15% in 4Q2026 versus 9% in 2Q2026, driven by Chinese AI spending and customers’ domestic-first procurement to secure supply. The bank said Huahong is expanding capacity with utilization remaining high amid strong demand and technology-node migration; generative AI is the main demand driver, lifting embedded flash, NOR flash and power-management IC demand. Huahong completed a September acquisition of a 97.5% stake in Huali Microelectronics; Huali has since become a wholly owned subsidiary and will be consolidated, which Goldman says broadens Huahong’s 12-inch manufacturing platform and supports long-term capacity and node expansion. Goldman expects strong demand to underpin utilization, ASPs and gross margins, notes Fab9A capacity will rise by about 40% by end-3Q2026 with full load targeted in 1H2027, and reiterates Buy with a 12-month PT of HK$335.

### 2026-09-22 13:22:24  #宏觀

【生態環境部：四大核心舉措保障農業灌溉用水安全】 9月22日，生態環境部召開9月例行新聞釋出會，生態環境部新聞發言人裴曉菲表示，八部門聯手出臺檔案，主要有四方面的舉措：一是抓好空間管控。從專案源頭減少汙染物進入農田，做到防患於未然；二是強化汙水排放管控。對農田周邊涉水汙染源開展排查，建立問題清單，拉條掛賬，制定整治方案並依法依規推進有序整改；三是加強水質監測。對於工業園區周邊的灌溉用水，灌溉期原則上至少開展2次監測，包括臨播種前、作物生長關鍵期；四是強化執法監管。堅決打擊汙染環境犯罪，依法查處違規設定排汙口，私設暗管排汙等違法行為，重點整治利用溶洞、滲坑、裂隙等非法排放工業廢水、畜禽糞汙等行為。

【摩根大通CEO：超大規模雲服務商的AI支出明年可能達到1萬億美元】摩根大通CEO傑米·戴蒙表示，人工智慧領域的支出熱潮幾乎沒有放緩跡象，超大規模雲服務商（hyperscaler）生態系統中的投資總額明年可能達到1萬億美元。該生態系統內的支出已從去年的約3000億美元激增至今年的約7000億美元，增加了一倍以上。戴蒙指出，這種激增在推動經濟增長的同時，也可能加劇通脹壓力。“這相當於每年推動GDP增長1%，”隨著企業招聘員工、建設工廠和發電廠以及採購裝置與材料，這些支出“可能會在一定程度上推高通脹”。不過，戴蒙表示，從長遠來看，人工智慧可能會產生通縮效應；他稱之為一項“令人難以置信的技術”，並指出其快速擴張的勢頭“似乎還將持續”。

### 2026-09-22 13:13:25  #其他

【工信部旗下媒體：“去寧德化”論調要不得】工信部旗下媒體中國工信新聞22日釋出文章《“去寧德化”論調要不得》。文章指出，最近一段時間，“去寧德化”在新能源汽車行業被反覆炒作，儼然成了一場輿論鬧劇。中國汽車產業鏈不是“太強了”，而是好不容易在一些關鍵環節建立起優勢，同時整體實力仍有繼續提升的巨大空間。在這樣的背景下，輿論如果動輒鼓吹“去某某化”，把本土龍頭企業與產業鏈其他企業人為對立起來，並不符合中國汽車產業今天所處的發展階段。一個成熟產業真正需要的，不是削峰填谷，更不是“誰領先就削弱誰”，而是既要有更多優秀企業成長，也要讓已經形成全球競爭力的企業繼續向前。

現實中，一些企業所謂“電池自研”，主要集中在電池包設計、系統整合、BMS管理甚至產品定義層面，與真正完成電芯研發並實現大規模穩定製造，並不是同一個概念。目前不少車企的相關佈局尚未深入到電芯製造核心環節。

動力電池本身就是資本、技術、人才和製造高度密集的產業。規模化製造尤其需要長期的工藝積累和質量控制，絕不是建起廠房、買來裝置，就自然形成產業競爭力。如果每一家整車企業都追求“大而全”，每一個環節都重複投資，每一種能力都要求內部解決，看似增加了自主性，實際上可能帶來新的低水平重複建設和資源錯配。

現代工業的真正競爭力，恰恰建立在專業化分工之上。我們當然需要更多動力電池企業成長，需要更多技術路線競爭，也需要整車企業增強自身技術能力。但“多元化”與“去龍頭化”不是一回事，“自主創新”與“什麼都自己做”也不是一回事。

產業競爭可以激烈，技術路線可以不同，供應鏈也可以多元。但有一條底線不能模糊：不能讓低質量內卷損害中國新能源產業來之不易的競爭優勢，不能讓營銷概念替代真正的技術創新，更不能把產業鏈協同人為變成產業鏈對立。

---

Finnish Jobless Rate Falls to 11-Month Low — Finland’s unemployment rate declined to 9.1% in August 2026 from 9.3% in the same month last year, marking the lowest level since September 2025. The number of unemployed decreased by 5,000 from a year earlier to 262,000, comprising 140,000 men and 122,000 women. By gender, the male unemployment rate remained at 9.2%, while the female rate fell to 8.9%, 0.5 percentage points lower than a year earlier. Among younger workers aged 15–24, the unemployment rate rose to 16.9%, which was 0.3 percentage points higher than a year earlier. Meanwhile, employment increased by 34,000 to 2.628 million, bringing the labour force participation rate to 69.4% from 68.7% a year earlier. The employment rate for those aged 20–64 also climbed to 77.3% from 76.2%.

【Unions step up recognition bids after UK easens collective-bargaining rules】The Labour government earlier this year eased collective-bargaining rules. Since April 6, after a worker-rights package lowered the threshold for recognition applications, unions have lodged 70 cases with the Central Arbitration Committee seeking formal bargaining rights at UK employers. That is more than double the number in the same period in 2025 and exceeds any comparable period since at least 2015. The cases filed over the past five months surpass the full-year totals in most years of the past decade. Where employers refuse voluntary recognition, unions may apply to the CAC. Employers fear the change could revive 1970s–80s-style bargaining, increasing negotiation workloads and strike-related productivity risks, says Andrew Moore, employment partner at Addleshaw Goddard.

Rupiah Inches Up as BI Signals FX Support — The Indonesian rupiah edged higher to near IDR 17,850 per U.S. dollar on Tuesday, snapping an eight-session losing streak after Bank Indonesia pledged intervention to ensure orderly trading. Officials said tools would include offshore and domestic NDFs, spot FX transactions, and secondary-market bond purchases. The central bank began its two-day policy meeting today, with local media and analysts widely expecting interest rates to stay unchanged as policymakers lean on FX operations and central bank securities to steady the currency. Borrowing costs have been held for two months after 100bps of hikes since May. On the fiscal side, Finance Minister Suahasil Nazara urged constructive debate over the statutory 3% deficit cap, following lawmakers’ push to revise the 2003 State Finance Law to allow wider shortfalls for social programs. Gains were constrained, however, by a firmer U.S. dollar near a two-month high, as hawkish Fed remarks supported higher-for-longer rate expectations.

【ECB chief economist LANE: new energy shock to make inflation more persistent】ECB chief economist LANE said a renewed energy-price shock, now affecting both oil and gas, will keep euro-area inflation higher and more persistent than the ECB initially expected. He said the second wave of price rises should push inflation up and extend its duration, with inflation only expected to fall back toward the ECB’s target from mid-2027, assuming the shock does not worsen. LANE warned a stronger, more persistent autumn shock would put pressure on the economy; if the shock is milder, large public spending in parts of Europe and some corporate AI activity should help sustain growth. His baseline: if the energy shock does not intensify, the euro-area economy should continue to grow at a steady but moderate pace.

Dutch Consumer Mood Least Negative in 6 Months — Consumer confidence in the Netherlands slightly improved to -33 in September 2026 from -34 in August, marking the least pessimistic reading since March but remaining well below its long-term average of -12 over the past 20 years. The improvement was driven by greater willingness to buy, which rose to -17 from -19 as consumers became more optimistic about their financial outlook for the next 12 months, with the balance rising to 0 from -3, while views on the timing of major purchases improved to -35 from -37. In contrast, the economic climate indicator fell to -58 from -57 as consumers became more negative about both the economic situation over the past 12 months, with the balance declining to -65 from -64, and the outlook for the next 12 months, which slipped to -50 from -49. Assessments of personal finances over the past year remained unchanged at -16. Despite the modest improvement, confidence also remained far below the record high of +36 reached in January 2000.

Palm Oil Rebounds After Recent Weakness — Malaysian palm oil futures strengthened, hovering above MYR 4,850 per tonne and snapping recent declines. Firmer soyoils on the Chicago markets supported sentiment, along with elevated crude oil prices as geopolitical risks in the Middle East persisted. Expectations of tighter supplies also lent support, as Indonesia’s B50 biodiesel mandate is set to divert more palm oil to domestic use, while El Niño risks could curb output in both Indonesia and Malaysia. Demand prospects improved as palm oil imports by top consumer India rose 7% from July to 782,761 tonnes in August, the highest since February, as refiners replenished stocks ahead of the festival season. Separately, Malaysia raised its October crude palm oil reference price while keeping the export duty at 10%. However, gains were tempered by weakness on the Dalian exchanges and signs of sluggish exports, with cargo surveyors estimating Malaysian palm oil product shipments fell between 12.8%–24.7% mom during September 1–20.

【自然資源部：“十五五”時期我國重要礦產資源需求仍將保持剛性增長】9月22日，自然資源部新聞發言人、副部長周星在國新辦新聞釋出會上表示，中國是製造業大國，“十五五”時期，我國重要礦產資源需求仍將保持剛性增長。為此，自然資源部將繼續實施新一輪找礦突破戰略行動，牢牢端穩能源這個飯碗，為我國發展先進製造業提供堅強保障。包括，實施國家地質填圖計劃，推動陸域1:50000區域地質調查覆蓋率提升至51%，重要成礦區帶礦產地質調查覆蓋率提升至66%；加大勘查開採的力度，將組織開展找礦大會戰行動，聚焦石油、鐵、銅、鋁等緊缺礦產，兼顧稀土等優勢礦產，部署一批重點勘查開採專案，穩步推進約300個能源資源基地建設，增強保障能源資源安全的底氣；加強科技創新，實現增量突破和存量盤活，推動礦業企業提高“三率”（開採回採率、選礦回收率和資源利用綜合利用率）水平等。

South Korean Won Rebounds — The South Korean won strengthened to around 1,360 per dollar, rebounding from four-week lows as oil prices fell sharply. The US 10-year Treasury yield also fell back below 5% after reaching recent highs last week, easing demand for the dollar. Additionally, South Korea’s strong export performance continued to underpin the won, with exports surging 78% year-on-year in the first 20 days of September, driven by a 259% jump in semiconductor shipments and boosting dollar inflows. Meanwhile, the dollar remained relatively firm as markets monitored the possibility of another Federal Reserve rate increase later this year. Separately, the Bank of Korea said it would assess inflation, growth and financial stability when determining the timing and pace of further rate hikes.

UK Natgas Prices Rise Following Steep Decline — UK natural gas prices rose above 183 pence per therm on Tuesday, after sliding more than 8% to a two-week low in the previous session on renewed diplomatic efforts to end the Middle East war and improving vessel flows. Investors are awaiting developments on potential talks between US President Trump and Iranian President Pezeshkian this week, as any progress toward resolving the conflict could reduce fears of prolonged disruptions to energy flows through the Strait of Hormuz. Meanwhile, reports of increasing numbers of LNG tankers and crude carriers finding alternative routes out of the Gulf have also helped ease supply concerns. Still, supply risks remain elevated, with Europe's gas storage levels remaining below the seasonal average at a time when they would typically be close to maximum capacity.

Copper Extends Rally to Sixth Session — Copper futures rose above $6.70 per pound on Tuesday, marking a sixth straight session of gains as supply concerns continued to support prices. Sprott Asset Management said global mined copper output could decline this year for the first time since 2017, despite record prices, due to production disruptions, lower ore grades and weak Chilean output. Global mine production fell 1.1% year over year in the first half of 2026, while disruptions at major mines in Indonesia and the Democratic Republic of Congo cut an estimated 600,000 tonnes from expected annual output. At the same time, demand remains strong from power grids, AI data centers and defense. Traders are also watching US copper tariff plans, with the Commerce Department proposing duties of 15% from January 2027 and 30% from 2028, pending a presidential decision.

【多找礦、找大礦、找富礦 未來五年重要部署】國務院新聞辦公室舉行“開局起步‘十五五’”系列主題新聞釋出會，介紹促進自然資源安全高效永續利用有關情況。相關負責人介紹，中國是製造業大國。“十五五”時期，我國重要礦產資源需求仍將保持剛性增長。自然資源部將繼續實施新一輪找礦突破戰略行動，牢牢端穩能源“飯碗”，為我國發展先進製造業提供堅強保障。堅持地質調查先行，打牢找礦工作基礎。礦一般賦存在地下，找礦首先要查清我國的基礎地質條件，圈出可能有礦的區域。為此，我們將組織實施國家地質填圖計劃，推動陸域1∶5萬區域地質調查覆蓋率提升至51%，重要成礦區帶礦產地質調查覆蓋率提升至66%，摸清資源能源家底。加大勘查開採力度，推動儲量增加、產能提升。我們將組織開展找礦大會戰行動，聚焦石油、鐵、銅、鋁等緊缺礦產，兼顧稀土等優勢礦產，部署一批重點勘查開採專案，力爭新發現一批大中型礦產地和油氣田，多找礦、找大礦、找富礦，同時穩步推進約300個能源資源基地建設，顯著提升我國戰略性礦產資源的自給率，增強保障能源資源安全的底氣。

【國家發改委：北京交通大學等在京高校雄安校區預計明年9月開門辦學】9月22日，國家發展改革委政策研究室副主任李超在發改委9月份新聞釋出會上表示，“雙一流”高校新校區建設是很多家庭都高度關注的問題。按照“十五五”規劃綱要部署要求，“十五五”時期，將有序擴大優質本科教育招生規模和研究生培養規模，支援建設若干“雙一流”高校新校區。近年來，我們透過中央預算內投資、超長期特別國債等政策資金，推動超過30個“雙一流”高校新校區進入大規模建設階段，改善高校基本辦學條件，佈局重大教學科研平臺，積極應對高等教育學齡人口達峰，支撐科技自主創新和人才自主培養。在新校區建設過程中，國家發展改革委將統籌謀劃高等教育與就業、產業、城市協同發展，力爭在更好滿足民生期待的同時，更好發揮高等教育對經濟社會高質量發展的支撐作用。一方面，推動新校區建設融入區域重大戰略。促進高等教育資源佈局與區域功能定位、發展需求相銜接。比如，支援北京交通大學、北京科技大學、北京林業大學、中國地質大學（北京）等在京高校向雄安新區疏解、規劃建設新校區，就是貫徹落實京津冀協同發展戰略的重要舉措。目前，相關校區建設已進入攻堅階段，預計將在明年9月開門辦學；另一方面，推動新校區建設與城市發展精準對接。統籌教育科研、居住生活、商業服務、公共文化等功能配套，更好促進人才集聚、職住平衡、產城融合，實現高校與城市功能互促、發展共進。比如，“兩重”建設支援的“雙一流”高校新校區專案中，約有50%的專案分佈在中西部、東北地區，將有效改善我國高等教育區域佈局，帶動中西部地區經濟社會發展。與此同時，國家發展改革委將始終堅持投資於物和投資於人緊密結合，注重補齊高校學生宿舍、食堂、體育、圖書館等設施短板，改善大學生學習生活環境，為廣大師生安心從教、潛心向學營造良好環境。

### 2026-09-22 10:36:01  #公司 #市場

【阿里巴巴：機器智慧時代，堅定投入AI模型 AI晶片 AI雲三大基石】在2026杭州雲棲大會上，阿里巴巴集團CEO吳泳銘表示，機器正在成為思考的主力，思考將像工業革命中的“動力”一樣，成為規模化供給的商品。未來機器思考總量將是人類的1000倍，而今天這一比例不到3%，至少還有幾萬倍的增長空間。吳泳銘認為，今天機器動力已經承擔了99.9%的物理工作，未來人類的思考總量還會繼續增長，但機器思考將以更快的速度擴張，預期將會承擔99.9%的思考工作。頂級思考將從奢侈品變成可規模化供給的商品，每個細分領域都可以擁有上百萬量級的AI科學家和專家，將給人類社會帶來巨大的改變。機器思考能夠規模化供給的前提，是AI基礎設施足夠完善、足夠發達，就像當年各類電器得以普及，第一步是建設足夠多的發電站和電網。1900年前後，已有不少電器進入家庭，當時全世界一整年的發電量，放到今天只夠用兩個小時。吳泳銘表示，AI模型、AI晶片和AI雲是機器智慧時代的三大基石，阿里巴巴將堅定投入這三項基礎設施建設，這是阿里的長期戰略選擇。（新浪科技）

### 2026-09-22 10:33:42  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Corn Holds Near 3-Year Peak — Corn futures held above $5.4 per bushel after hitting their highest level since July 2023, supported by strong US export demand, tightening global supplies and expectations for further agricultural trade with China. US corn export inspections jumped 40% year-on-year to 1.94 million tonnes in the week ended September 17, exceeding market expectations and lifting 2026/27 shipments further ahead of last year’s pace. Heavy Midwest rains are also delaying harvest in parts of the region, limiting the near-term flow of new supplies, although overall harvesting remains slightly ahead of the five-year average. Global fundamentals remain supportive, with USDA projecting a nearly 30-million-tonne shortfall between world corn production and consumption in 2026/27, the largest deficit in more than three decades. Traders are also watching the Trump-Xi meeting this week for potential progress on US agricultural purchases, while continued disruptions to Black Sea grain trade are adding support.

Platinum Futures Rangebound — Platinum futures traded around $1,800 an ounce, moving in a tight range as a firmer US dollar offset easing inflationary pressures from retreating oil prices. Oil prices continued to decline amid signs of increased diplomatic efforts to resolve the conflict and restore energy flows in the Middle East. This eased concerns over inflation and the prospect of further interest rate hikes, lowering the opportunity cost of holding platinum. However, hawkish comments from Federal Reserve officials reinforced expectations for a tighter US interest-rate outlook following last week’s first rate hike in three years. Underlying fundamentals remained supportive, with WPIC forecasting a 5% increase in industrial demand in 2026, partly driven by AI infrastructure, although automotive demand is forecast to fall 4%. Despite a projected 265k oz surplus in 2026, WPIC also expects above-ground stocks to remain at only about 3.4 months of global demand, following the large deficits of previous years.

【華立汪力成：跳出內卷、摒棄規模擴張思維是中國民企的當務之急]“全球化不會終止，但是它的形態、內容以及定義正在被重新改寫。低關稅甚至零關稅的全球化合作已經終止，國家與國家之間的大分工也已經終止，全球的供應鏈正在向分散式、區域化、近岸外包等方向演變。”9月21日，華立集團董事局主席汪力成，在長江商學院舉辦的活動上發言，他認為近幾年中國出口不降反增，是由於中國已經形成的齊全、完整、高效的製造體系，發達國家市場暫時無法輕易“脫鏈斷鏈”，但此類趨勢沒有改變，所以中國企業仍需提前做好應對準備。 最近一年，“K型分化”成為宏觀經濟領域的高頻熱詞，其主要指以AI為代表的新興產業持續向上，但傳統行業可能持續向下的分化。汪力成認為，K型分化是表象，本質上是經濟結構正在重構——靠傳統三駕馬車（消費、投資、出口）拉動經濟增長的模式難以為繼；隨著房地產業的“崩盤”，大量的相關產業萎縮趨勢不會改變；政府的土地財政模式已經終結，依附在其他產業上的稅負會進一步增加。(財新網)

### 2026-09-22 09:50:46  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Currency #Importance 1

Yen Remains on Intervention Watch — The Japanese yen weakened to around 157.5 per dollar on Tuesday, falling for a third straight session and keeping traders alert for possible intervention amid an extended holiday in Japan. Tokyo has previously taken advantage of thin holiday liquidity to intervene in currency markets, while concerns intensified following reports that the Bank of Japan conducted a rate check with market participants late Friday. The yen also remained under pressure from a stronger dollar as hawkish comments from Federal Reserve officials reinforced expectations for further US interest rate hikes. Last week, the Japanese currency fell sharply after the BOJ raised rates in a widely anticipated move, with two policymakers dissenting from the decision. Governor Kazuo Ueda said the BOJ remains committed to raising rates and adjusting the degree of monetary accommodation as economic conditions evolve, while noting that accommodative financial conditions are expected to remain in place to support growth.
